Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the black_holes framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 213. The resulting operator achieves bound O(515/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to black_holes theory, we decompose the problem into 213 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 515. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
